# CNC Twin validation report

Generated by `twin validate` (`cnctwin.validation`, see docs/validation.md) from the cases in `validation/cases/` against the budgets of `validation/budgets.yaml`. Every case is simulated by its quantity's harness with the domain libraries at the level the experiment measured; models take every parameter from the databases and the case's stated inputs (nothing is tuned to a reference). Errors are relative to the reference (temperatures: to the rise above ambient). Cases whose reference also built a database value the model used are **calibration-dependent** and are reported separately; verdicts use the independent cases only.

## Summary

* **Pass**: none.
* **Fail**: chip_form, chip_ratio, chip_temperature, coolant_reach, cutting_force, cycle_time, drilling_thrust, drilling_torque, roughness, shear_angle, spindle_growth, stability_lobe, taylor_life, tool_temperature, wall_error.
* Not judged, no budget in validation/budgets.yaml: none.
* No harness yet: none.

Budget rule (validation/budgets.yaml, applied as written): tolerance = max(budget_pct/100 x |reference| (for temperatures: x the rise above ambient), 2 x the reference's uncertainty); a case passes when every compared value lies within its tolerance; a quantity passes when at least 80 % of its cases pass and no case misses by more than twice its tolerance. Values without a prediction count as misses; values marked 'not compared' are listed with the reason but do not count. The error statistics are over the compared scalar values of the independent cases (reference values and curve points; temperatures as rises above ambient); classifications are counted as matches.

Calibration-dependent cases (excluded from the verdicts):

* `chipform-tungaloy-cnmg120408-tm-steel` (chip_form): tool tungaloy-cnmg120408-tm-t9215-pclnr2525m12e chip breaker window from `tungaloy-2023-gc-insert`

## chip_form

* Budget: 25 % (chip form class or chip grade (match / mismatch, the percentage does not apply); relative error of chip-control window boundaries (feed, depth of cut) and of chip dimensions).
* Verdict: **fail** - only 0 of 2 cases within budget (0% < 80%); chipform-slodki2019-cnmg120404-sf-ti64eli-hpc misses by inf tolerances (> 2).
* Harness: `cnctwin.validation.harnesses.chipform.chip_form`.
* Oxley chip state and cnctwin.mechanics.chipform (curl, breaker window, Nakayama breaking, ISO 3685 class, piece length) per tested feed and depth; the length is mapped to the case's own classes, or a feed-depth grid gives the chip-control window.

| group | cases | cases within budget | counted values (no prediction) | median abs. error (all / values / curve points) | p90 abs. error | class matches | values within uncertainty | largest miss (tolerances) |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| independent | 2 | 0/2 | 32 (0) | - / - / - | - | 5/32 | - | inf (chipform-slodki2019-cnmg120404-sf-ti64eli-hpc) |
| calibration-dependent | 1 | 0/1 | 9 (0) | 75.4 % / 74.7 % / 75.4 % | 91.8 % | - | 0/9 | 5.54 (chipform-tungaloy-cnmg120408-tm-steel) |

**Failures and likely causes.** 0 of 2 independent cases within budget. Values within tolerance per case: chipform-slodki2019-cnmg120404-sf-ti64eli-hpc 4/16; chipform-zebala2020-cnmg120408-smc-ti64-dry-hpc 1/16. The chip-form model's breaking criterion and curl radii are estimates (mechanics.md 13) and the case inserts' breaker geometries (land, groove width and depth) are mostly not in the database, so the comparison tests the defaults.

### chipform-slodki2019-cnmg120404-sf-ti64eli-hpc

* Source: `slodki-2019-ti64-eli-hpc-turning`, Sect. 2 (material, insert, holder, coolant), Tables 2-3 (plan with vc per trial), Sect. 3 (grading rule), Table 9 (grade and cut cross-section per feed and depth); Table 8 holds the chip photographs (measured).
* File: `validation/cases/chip_form/chipform-slodki2019-cnmg120404-sf-ti64eli-hpc.yaml`; operation longitudinal_turning; material `ti6al4v`.
* Validation class: **independent**.
* Model chain: cnctwin.mechanics.oxley.solve -> cnctwin.mechanics.chipform.chip_form.
* Settings: breaking_codes = [22, 23, 31, 32, 42, 43, 51, 52, 61, 62, 70], coating = none, db_insert = -, edge_radius_um = 35, entering_angle_deg = 95, rake_deg = -6, tool_material = carbide-k20-6co.
* Inputs the case marks as assumed: `setup.coolant.delivery` = through the PCLNR2020K12HP high-pressure holder onto the rake face (the paper names the HP holder and the pressure but not the nozzle size, flow or jet angle).
* Inputs the harness assumed: ambient temperature = 20 degC (case states none (or marks it assumed): ISO 1 reference temperature, est); tool material = carbide-k20-6co (db/tool_materials/carbide-k20-6co.yaml names Sandvik H13A (straight WC-6Co), db/tool_materials); chip breaker geometry = none (natural curl) (the SF breaker's land and groove are not in the database, db/tools); rake and inclination = -6 / -6 deg (not stated: a negative ISO holder for a negative insert, est); entering angle = 95 deg (not stated: the C-insert holders named (PCLNR, PCMNN) are 95 deg holders, est); cutting-edge radius = 35 um (not stated in the case: the tooling model's default for this tool (insert_edge_radius_m), db/models/tooling.yaml); coolant emulsion-8pct = friction factor 0.83 (the case's emulsion is not in the database: the database emulsion, full reach of the jet at the rake face, db/coolants).
* Database values used: tool material carbide-k20-6co thermal properties (`mitsubishi-2026-tm102g`); ti6al4v flow stress (Johnson-Cook) (`ducobu-2017-jc-ti6al4v`); ti6al4v physical.density_kg_m3 (`aubert-duval-2018-ta6v`); ti6al4v physical.specific_heat_J_kgK (`upmet-2026-ti64-eli`); ti6al4v physical.conductivity_W_mK (`upmet-2026-ti64-eli`); ti6al4v cutting.min_chip_thickness_ratio (`wojciechowski-2022-min-chip-thickness`); ti6al4v friction mu_sliding (carbide, uncoated) (`klippel-2024-ck45-orthogonal`).
* Material: database entry ti6al4v; the case says: Grade 5 ELI, hot-forged, annealed and peeled bar D 50 mm; Rm 902 MPa, 29 HRC; Al 6.10, V 4.13, Fe 0.05, O 0.10 % (Table 1)
* High-pressure coolant enters as a friction factor; the jet's chip-breaking action is not modelled.
* Result: 16 compared, 0 without prediction, 0 not compared; 4/16 classes match; case **outside budget**; largest miss inf tolerances.
* Likely cause: 12 of 16 compared values miss. Without the breaker's groove geometry the model curls the chip with its natural radius (15 x contact length, est) and breaks it only when the bending strain t2/2R exceeds 0.1 x the bulk fracture strain (est): it predicts unbroken chips everywhere; the HPC jet's breaking action is not modelled.

### chipform-tungaloy-cnmg120408-tm-steel

* Source: `tungaloy-2023-gc-turning-us`, p. B057 (PDF p. 65), 'Chip control range' chart for P, TM outline (digitised); p. B004 (PDF p. 12), standard cutting conditions for TM (measured, digitised, stated uncertainty 2 %).
* File: `validation/cases/chip_form/chipform-tungaloy-cnmg120408-tm-steel.yaml`; operation longitudinal_turning; material `aisi1045`.
* Validation class: **calibration-dependent**: tool tungaloy-cnmg120408-tm-t9215-pclnr2525m12e chip breaker window from `tungaloy-2023-gc-insert` (source).
* Model chain: cnctwin.mechanics.oxley.solve -> cnctwin.mechanics.chipform.chip_form.
* Settings: breaking_codes = [22, 23, 31, 32, 42, 43, 51, 52, 61, 62, 70], coating = al2o3-cvd, db_insert = tungaloy-cnmg120408-tm-t9215-pclnr2525m12e, edge_radius_um = 35, entering_angle_deg = 95, rake_deg = 0, tool_material = carbide-p30.
* Inputs the case marks as assumed: `setup.conditions.coolant` = emulsion (not stated); `setup.conditions.cutting_speed_m_min` = 250 (the chart states no speed; middle of the B004 range for TM / T9215 in medium carbon steels).
* Inputs the harness assumed: ambient temperature = 20 degC (case states none (or marks it assumed): ISO 1 reference temperature, est); rake and inclination = +0 / -6 deg working (seat -6 deg + chip breaker +6 deg) (as the twin builds the insert in its holder (tooling.turning: the holder's stated seat, else the negative-holder defaults; the element's normal rake is seat + breaker), db/tools); entering angle = 95 deg (not stated: the C-insert holders named (PCLNR, PCMNN) are 95 deg holders, est); cutting-edge radius = 35 um (not stated in the case: the tooling model's default for this tool (insert_edge_radius_m), db/models/tooling.yaml).
* Database values used: tool tungaloy-cnmg120408-tm-t9215-pclnr2525m12e chip breaker window (`tungaloy-2023-gc-insert`); aisi1045 flow stress (Johnson-Cook) (`burns-2012-1045-cutting-temperature`); aisi1045 physical.density_kg_m3 (`xiu-2019-1045-grinding-fe`); aisi1045 physical.specific_heat_J_kgK (`xiu-2019-1045-grinding-fe`); aisi1045 physical.conductivity_W_mK (`xiu-2019-1045-grinding-fe`); aisi1045 cutting.min_chip_thickness_ratio (`est`); aisi1045 friction mu_sliding (carbide, uncoated) (`est`).
* Material: database entry aisi1045; the case says: the chart is for ISO group P without a named steel; B004 lists 1045 and 4140 as 'medium carbon steels, alloy steels' for the TM conditions
* Insert from the database: tungaloy-cnmg120408-tm-t9215-pclnr2525m12e (chip breaker TM, window and land/rake of the maker's catalogue).
* Result: 9 compared, 0 without prediction, 0 not compared; median abs. error 75.4 %, max 138.6 %; 0/9 within uncertainty; case **outside budget**; largest miss 5.54 tolerances.
* Likely cause: 9 of 9 compared values miss. Without the breaker's groove geometry the model curls the chip with its natural radius (15 x contact length, est) and breaks it only when the bending strain t2/2R exceeds 0.1 x the bulk fracture strain (est): it predicts unbroken chips everywhere; the HPC jet's breaking action is not modelled. The case counts snarled tubular and washer chips (2.3, 4.3) and long conical helices (5.1) as controlled; the model's unbroken chips fall in these classes at every grid point, so the predicted window is the whole grid.

* Result: 16 compared, 0 without prediction, 0 not compared; 1/16 classes match; case **outside budget**; largest miss inf tolerances.
* Likely cause: 15 of 16 compared values miss. Without the breaker's groove geometry the model curls the chip with its natural radius (15 x contact length, est) and breaks it only when the bending strain t2/2R exceeds 0.1 x the bulk fracture strain (est): it predicts unbroken chips everywhere; the HPC jet's breaking action is not modelled.

## chip_ratio

* Budget: 20 % (relative error of the simulated chip compression ratio h / h_chip (mean chip thickness for segmented chips)).
* Verdict: **fail** - only 0 of 3 cases within budget (0% < 80%); chipratio-orth-ck45-klippel2022-vc150 misses by inf tolerances (> 2).
* Harness: `cnctwin.validation.harnesses.orthogonal.chip_ratio`.
* Oxley's orthogonal theory (cnctwin.mechanics.oxley.solve) per test at t1 = h - h_min, tool and work at ambient; simulated ratio h / t2 against the measured h / h_chip.

| group | cases | cases within budget | counted values (no prediction) | median abs. error (all / values / curve points) | p90 abs. error | class matches | values within uncertainty | largest miss (tolerances) |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| independent | 3 | 0/3 | 54 (2) | 26.4 % / 26.4 % / - | 49.1 % | - | 1/5 | inf (chipratio-orth-ck45-klippel2022-vc150) |
| calibration-dependent | 2 | 0/2 | 10 (0) | 41.9 % / 41.9 % / - | 61.7 % | - | 1/10 | 3.18 (chipratio-orth-ti64-klippel2022-vc70) |

**Failures and likely causes.** 0 of 3 independent cases within budget. Values within tolerance per case: chipratio-orth-ck45-klippel2022-f010 1/15; chipratio-orth-ck45-klippel2022-vc150 10/15; chipratio-orth-ss304-marcicano2005 6/24. Over the independent cases the simulated chip ratio is on average -14 % off (positive: the simulated chip is thinner than measured). With the Coulomb-Tresca interface in the sliding regime, Oxley's shear angle is set mainly by the friction angle (lambda = atan mu) and the strain-hardening term, so the simulated ratio barely changes with speed and feed while the measured one varies. The friction coefficients of the material files are estimates for Ck45 and 304; BUE (Ck45 below 70 m/min) and the ploughed layer h_min (an estimated ratio x the edge radius) decide the low-speed and small-h tests, where the model forms no chip at h = 10 um although the experiment did. The two Ti6Al4V cases are calibration-dependent (Johnson-Cook set chosen against the Ducobu experiments; friction from the ETH tests) and are reported apart. No coefficient was changed for this report.

### chipratio-orth-ck45-klippel2022-f010

* Source: `klippel-2022-arxiv-orthogonal-data`, Table B.15 (Ck45), column havg, tests V0072, V0073, V0074, V0125, V0162, V0084, V0085, V0093, V0094, V0095, V0236, V0245, V0293, V0299, V0300 (measured).
* File: `validation/cases/chip_ratio/chipratio-orth-ck45-klippel2022-f010.yaml`; operation orthogonal_turning; material `aisi1045`.
* Validation class: **independent**.
* Model chain: cnctwin.mechanics.materials.WorkMaterial/ToolSide (databases) -> cnctwin.mechanics.contact.min_chip_thickness -> cnctwin.mechanics.oxley.solve (Oxley 1989 with Johnson-Cook, Coulomb-Tresca interface, rake heat partition at the tool temperature).
* Settings: coating = none, h_min_ratio = 0.27, mu_sliding = 0.5, tool_material = carbide-k20-6co, tool_temperature = ambient (tool body at the start of a short test), work_temperature_K = 293.1.
* Inputs the harness assumed: tool material = carbide-k20-6co (db/tool_materials/carbide-k20-6co.yaml names Sandvik H13A (straight WC-6Co), db/tool_materials); ambient temperature = 20 degC (case states none (or marks it assumed): ISO 1 reference temperature, est).
* Database values used: tool material carbide-k20-6co thermal properties (`mitsubishi-2026-tm102g`); aisi1045 flow stress (Johnson-Cook) (`burns-2012-1045-cutting-temperature`); aisi1045 physical.density_kg_m3 (`xiu-2019-1045-grinding-fe`); aisi1045 physical.specific_heat_J_kgK (`xiu-2019-1045-grinding-fe`); aisi1045 physical.conductivity_W_mK (`xiu-2019-1045-grinding-fe`); aisi1045 cutting.min_chip_thickness_ratio (`est`); aisi1045 friction mu_sliding (carbide, uncoated) (`est`).
* Material: database entry aisi1045; the case says: Ck45 (C45E) rolled bar, one batch for all tests: supplier tensile test Rm 671 MPa, Rp0.2 420 MPa, A5 22.2 % (Table 2), ferritic-pearlitic; the outer scale and decarburised zone were turned off before the tests (Sect. 2.6). The DB entry is normalised AISI 1045 (+N, ~190 HB): use the batch strength above when calibrating flow stress.
* Result: 15 compared, 0 without prediction, 15 not compared; median abs. error 30.0 %, max 72.6 %; 0/2 within uncertainty; case **outside budget**; largest miss 3.63 tolerances.
* Likely cause: simulated ratio 0.186-0.544 against measured 0.241-0.679 (mean signed error -9 %); database sliding friction mu = 0.5, h_min ratio 0.27. Ck45 has a built-up edge at 10-50 m/min (database BUE window) that thickens the chip; the orthogonal solution has no BUE. The ETH data give an Albrecht friction coefficient of 0.78; the database keeps the estimate 0.5. With the Coulomb-Tresca interface in the sliding regime, Oxley's shear angle is set mainly by the friction angle (lambda = atan mu) and the strain-hardening term, so the simulated ratio barely changes with speed and feed while the measured one varies.

### chipratio-orth-ss304-marcicano2005

* Source: `marcicano-2005-aisi304-cutting-tests`, Tables 3 and 4, columns t0 and t1 (measured).
* File: `validation/cases/chip_ratio/chipratio-orth-ss304-marcicano2005.yaml`; operation orthogonal_turning; material `aisi304`.
* Validation class: **independent**.
* Model chain: cnctwin.mechanics.materials.WorkMaterial/ToolSide (databases) -> cnctwin.mechanics.contact.min_chip_thickness -> cnctwin.mechanics.oxley.solve (Oxley 1989 with Johnson-Cook, Coulomb-Tresca interface, rake heat partition at the tool temperature).
* Settings: coating = none, h_min_ratio = 0.27, mu_sliding = 0.6, tool_material = carbide-k20-6co, tool_temperature = ambient (tool body at the start of a short test), work_temperature_K = 293.1.
* Inputs the case marks as assumed: `setup.tool.clearance_deg` = 6 (assumed: not stated: ISO 1832 letter P = 11 deg insert clearance (code convention, standard not read) minus the 5 deg positive tilt that gives the stated rake); `setup.tool.edge_radius_um` = 15 (assumed: not stated; typical of a ground (G-tolerance) uncoated insert edge (est.)); `setup.tool.nose_radius_mm` = 0.8 (assumed: ISO code '08' = 0.8 mm corner radius (code convention); outside the cut when the tube wall lies on the straight edge); `setup.conditions.coolant` = dry (assumed: not stated; dry orthogonal tests are implied by the analysis).
* Inputs the harness assumed: tool material = carbide-k20-6co (grade not in the database: the carbide class default, db/tool_materials); ambient temperature = 20 degC (case states none (or marks it assumed): ISO 1 reference temperature, est).
* Database values used: tool material carbide-k20-6co thermal properties (`mitsubishi-2026-tm102g`); aisi304 flow stress (Johnson-Cook) (`zou-2026-304-min-cutting-thickness`); aisi304 physical.density_kg_m3 (`thyssenkrupp-2020-14301`); aisi304 physical.specific_heat_J_kgK (`thyssenkrupp-2020-14301`); aisi304 physical.conductivity_W_mK (`outokumpu-steel-grades`); aisi304 cutting.min_chip_thickness_ratio (`zou-2026-304-min-cutting-thickness`); aisi304 friction mu_sliding (carbide, uncoated) (`zou-2026-304-min-cutting-thickness`).
* Material: database entry aisi304; the case says: annealed AISI 304 tubes; quasi-static compression gives JC A 360 MPa, B 1244 MPa, n 0.72 (Table 1); hardness not stated
* Result: 24 compared, 0 without prediction, 24 not compared; median abs. error 33.0 %, max 54.1 %; case **outside budget**; largest miss 2.7 tolerances.
* Likely cause: simulated ratio 0.221-0.408 against measured 0.234-0.541 (mean signed error -27 %); database sliding friction mu = 0.6, h_min ratio 0.27. With the Coulomb-Tresca interface in the sliding regime, Oxley's shear angle is set mainly by the friction angle (lambda = atan mu) and the strain-hardening term, so the simulated ratio barely changes with speed and feed while the measured one varies.

### chipratio-orth-ti64-ducobu2017

* Source: `ducobu-2017-ti6al4v-orthogonal-reference`, Table 3 (experimental reference: average RMS forces per mm width and average chip thickness for h = 40, 60, 100 um) (measured, stated uncertainty 10 %).
* File: `validation/cases/chip_ratio/chipratio-orth-ti64-ducobu2017.yaml`; operation orthogonal_turning; material `ti6al4v`.
* Validation class: **calibration-dependent**: ti6al4v flow stress (Johnson-Cook) from `ducobu-2017-jc-ti6al4v` (source).
* Model chain: cnctwin.mechanics.materials.WorkMaterial/ToolSide (databases) -> cnctwin.mechanics.contact.min_chip_thickness -> cnctwin.mechanics.oxley.solve (Oxley 1989 with Johnson-Cook, Coulomb-Tresca interface, rake heat partition at the tool temperature).
* Settings: coating = none, h_min_ratio = 0.37, mu_sliding = 0.3, tool_material = carbide-k20-6co, tool_temperature = ambient (tool body at the start of a short test), work_temperature_K = 293.1.
* Inputs the case marks as assumed: `setup.tool.coating` = none (assumed: not stated; the FE model of the same paper uses uncoated tungsten carbide); `setup.conditions.coolant` = dry (assumed: not stated in this paper).
* Inputs the harness assumed: tool material = carbide-k20-6co (uncoated straight WC-Co: the straight WC-6Co K20 entry, db/tool_materials); ambient temperature = 20 degC (case states none (or marks it assumed): ISO 1 reference temperature, est).
* Database values used: tool material carbide-k20-6co thermal properties (`mitsubishi-2026-tm102g`); ti6al4v flow stress (Johnson-Cook) (`ducobu-2017-jc-ti6al4v`); ti6al4v physical.density_kg_m3 (`aubert-duval-2018-ta6v`); ti6al4v physical.specific_heat_J_kgK (`upmet-2026-ti64-eli`); ti6al4v physical.conductivity_W_mK (`upmet-2026-ti64-eli`); ti6al4v cutting.min_chip_thickness_ratio (`wojciechowski-2022-min-chip-thickness`); ti6al4v friction mu_sliding (carbide, uncoated) (`klippel-2024-ck45-orthogonal`).
* Material: database entry ti6al4v; the case says: Ti6Al4V; heat treatment, hardness and supplier data are not given in the source (DB entry: mill annealed); the paper's model uses E 113.8 GPa, rho 4430 kg/m3, k 7.3 W/mK, cp 580 J/kgK (Table 4)
* Result: 3 compared, 0 without prediction, 3 not compared; median abs. error 31.7 %, max 34.5 %; 0/3 within uncertainty; case **outside budget**; largest miss 1.73 tolerances.
* Likely cause: simulated ratio 0.485-0.558 against measured 0.678-0.75 (mean signed error -28 %); database sliding friction mu = 0.3, h_min ratio 0.37. Ti6Al4V forms segmented chips (database tendency 'segmented'); Oxley's continuous-chip solution over-predicts the thickness ratio of segmented chips (mechanics.md 13). With the Coulomb-Tresca interface in the sliding regime, Oxley's shear angle is set mainly by the friction angle (lambda = atan mu) and the strain-hardening term, so the simulated ratio barely changes with speed and feed while the measured one varies.

## chip_temperature

* Budget: 20 % (relative error of the simulated chip (free-surface) temperature rise above ambient).
* Verdict: **fail** - only 0 of 1 cases within budget (0% < 80%); chiptemp-orth-ck45-klippel2022-temper misses by 2.43 tolerances (> 2).
* Harness: `cnctwin.validation.harnesses.temperature.chip_temperature`.
* Oxley's mean chip temperature per test (t1 = h - h_min, tool at ambient), its oxide exposure during the contact and the air cooling afterwards (cnctwin.mechanics.colour), read as the equivalent temperature of the material's tempering-colour table and capped at its top row, as the experimenters' reading was.

| group | cases | cases within budget | counted values (no prediction) | median abs. error (all / values / curve points) | p90 abs. error | class matches | values within uncertainty | largest miss (tolerances) |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| independent | 1 | 0/1 | 24 (0) | 20.2 % / 20.2 % / - | 44.4 % | - | - | 2.43 (chiptemp-orth-ck45-klippel2022-temper) |

**Failures and likely causes.** 0 of 1 independent cases within budget. Values within tolerance per case: chiptemp-orth-ck45-klippel2022-temper 11/24. One indirect reference (tempering colours, capped at 360 C). The simulated reading is the colour-equivalent temperature of the chip's thermal history, so the oxide-growth estimates of mechanics-colour.yaml enter directly.

### chiptemp-orth-ck45-klippel2022-temper

* Source: `klippel-2022-arxiv-orthogonal-data`, Table B.15 (Ck45), column Tchip, tests V0072, V0073, V0074, V0123, V0124, V0125, V0160, V0161, V0162, V0084, V0085, V0093, V0094, V0095, V0236, V0245, V0246, V0247, V0292, V0293, V0294, V0298, V0299, V0300; Sect. 3.5 and Fig. 31 (measured).
* File: `validation/cases/chip_temperature/chiptemp-orth-ck45-klippel2022-temper.yaml`; operation orthogonal_turning; material `aisi1045`.
* Validation class: **independent**.
* Model chain: cnctwin.mechanics.oxley.solve -> cnctwin.mechanics.colour.emitted_chip_colour -> cnctwin.mechanics.colour.equivalent_temperature -> cnctwin.thermal.convection.
* Settings: coating = none, colour_table_top_degC = 360, mu_sliding = 0.5, oxide_activation_K = 1.684e+04, tool_material = carbide-k20-6co.
* Inputs the harness assumed: tool material = carbide-k20-6co (db/tool_materials/carbide-k20-6co.yaml names Sandvik H13A (straight WC-6Co), db/tool_materials); ambient temperature = 20 degC (case states none (or marks it assumed): ISO 1 reference temperature, est); chip cooling after the contact = free convection + radiation in still air (cnctwin.thermal.convection: horizontal cylinder of the chip width, emissivity 0.35 (steel), est).
* Database values used: tool material carbide-k20-6co thermal properties (`mitsubishi-2026-tm102g`); aisi1045 flow stress (Johnson-Cook) (`burns-2012-1045-cutting-temperature`); aisi1045 physical.density_kg_m3 (`xiu-2019-1045-grinding-fe`); aisi1045 physical.specific_heat_J_kgK (`xiu-2019-1045-grinding-fe`); aisi1045 physical.conductivity_W_mK (`xiu-2019-1045-grinding-fe`); aisi1045 cutting.min_chip_thickness_ratio (`est`); aisi1045 friction mu_sliding (carbide, uncoated) (`est`); aisi1045 chip.oxide_colours (`wikipedia-de-anlassen`).
* Material: database entry aisi1045; the case says: Ck45 (C45E) rolled bar, one batch for all tests: supplier tensile test Rm 671 MPa, Rp0.2 420 MPa, A5 22.2 % (Table 2), ferritic-pearlitic; the outer scale and decarburised zone were turned off before the tests (Sect. 2.6). The DB entry is normalised AISI 1045 (+N, ~190 HB): use the batch strength above when calibrating flow stress.
* Result: 24 compared, 0 without prediction, 0 not compared; median abs. error 20.2 %, max 48.5 %; case **outside budget**; largest miss 2.43 tolerances.
* Likely cause: mean signed error -8 % of the rise. The reading depends on the oxide model (activation energy est., colour table read as 30-min holds) as much as on the chip temperature; at the colour table's top the reference is only a lower bound.

## coolant_reach

* Budget: 15 % (relative error of the coolant-dependent quantity (tool-work interface temperature rise above ambient, cutting force) under the case's coolant; chip grades are classifications (class match)).
* Verdict: **fail** - only 0 of 2 cases within budget (0% < 80%); coolant-struzikiewicz2023-ti64-jet-direction misses by inf tolerances (> 2).
* Harness: `cnctwin.validation.harnesses.coolant.coolant_reach`.
* The case's jet traced through a minimal scene of its own geometry to the cut (cnctwin.coolant: reach and arrival), then the coolant's three actions on the measured quantity: lubrication (friction factor), convective cooling of the tool faces at the engagement (Martin, normal speed component) in thermal's tool ladder over the pass, and the hydraulic wedge of a jet running against the chip flow (shorter contact: friction force and natural chip curl).

| group | cases | cases within budget | counted values (no prediction) | median abs. error (all / values / curve points) | p90 abs. error | class matches | values within uncertainty | largest miss (tolerances) |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| independent | 2 | 0/2 | 63 (0) | 25.8 % / 25.8 % / - | 52.9 % | 10/18 | - | inf (coolant-struzikiewicz2023-ti64-jet-direction) |

**Failures and likely causes.** 0 of 2 independent cases within budget. Values within tolerance per case: coolant-struzikiewicz2023-ti64-jet-direction 10/36; coolant-sultana2023-ti64-endmill-hpc-vs-dry 17/27. The coolant now acts through the reach traced in each case's scene, the lubrication friction factor, the convective cooling of the tool faces at the engagement and the hydraulic wedge of a jet that runs under the chip; the dry levels of the mechanics set most of the remaining error.

### coolant-struzikiewicz2023-ti64-jet-direction

* Source: `struzikiewicz-2023-hpc-jet-direction`, Sect. 2 (setup), Fig. 1b (nozzle geometry: 0.7 mm bore, 20 mm distance, alpha/beta definition), Table 1 (material), Tables 3 and 5 (plan, Fc and Cch per test), Table 8 (chip-form grade) (measured).
* File: `validation/cases/coolant_reach/coolant-struzikiewicz2023-ti64-jet-direction.yaml`; operation longitudinal_turning; material `ti6al4v`.
* Validation class: **independent**.
* Model chain: cnctwin.coolant.occlusion (case scene: insert, holder, bar) -> cnctwin.coolant.lubrication.friction -> cnctwin.coolant.engagement (hydraulic wedge) -> cnctwin.mechanics.chipform.chip_form -> cnctwin.mechanics.coefficients (cutting force).
* Settings: edge_radius_um = 35, fluid = emulsion-8pct, h_min_um = 12.95, jet_diameter_mm = 0.605, jet_speed_m_s = 156.5, rake_deg = 9, zone_radius_mm = 1.5.
* Inputs the case marks as assumed: `setup.coolant.flow_l_min` = 2.7 (not stated; orifice estimate Q = Cd A sqrt(2 p / rho) with Cd 0.7, A of a 0.7 mm bore, p 150 bar, rho 1000 kg/m3).
* Inputs the harness assumed: ambient temperature = 20 degC (case states none (or marks it assumed): ISO 1 reference temperature, est); tool material = carbide-submicron-10co (Sandvik 1115: PVD-coated fine-grained S-grade substrate, db/tool_materials); coating = none (case coating 'PVD-coated grade (Sandvik 1115)' has no database entry: modelled uncoated, db/coatings); chip breaker geometry = none (natural curl) (the SGF breaker's land and groove are not in the database, db/tools); entering angle = 95 deg (not stated: the C-insert holders named (PCLNR, PCMNN) are 95 deg holders, est); cutting-edge radius = 35 um (not stated in the case: the tooling model's default for this tool (insert_edge_radius_m), db/models/tooling.yaml); rake = +9 deg working (the case's 15 deg rake land of the insert profile on a negative holder tilted -6 deg (the holder's tilt not stated: negative ISO holder), case); inclination = -6 deg (not stated: a negative ISO holder for a negative insert, est); jet direction = alpha = elevation above the rake face; beta = azimuth in the rake plane from the insert's corner bisector, positive toward the major cutting edge (the case defines beta from a reference line drawn only in its Fig. 1b; the bisector is the usual reference and the sign the only one that keeps the 20 mm nozzle outside the bar at alpha 30 deg, est); chip flow direction = normal to the chord of the engaged edge (Colwell 1954) (nose radius, entering angle, ap and feed as stated, case); occlusion scene = insert 12.7 mm IC x 4.76 mm (box below the rake face), holder shank 20 x 20 mm, bar D 50 mm stepped by ap (the case's insert, holder designation and stock; insert size from thermal's defaults (CNGG 1204: 12.7 x 4.76 mm), case, db/models/thermal-tool.yaml).
* Database values used: tool material carbide-submicron-10co thermal properties (`hyperion-insert-blanks-grades`); ti6al4v flow stress (Johnson-Cook) (`ducobu-2017-jc-ti6al4v`); ti6al4v physical.density_kg_m3 (`aubert-duval-2018-ta6v`); ti6al4v physical.specific_heat_J_kgK (`upmet-2026-ti64-eli`); ti6al4v physical.conductivity_W_mK (`upmet-2026-ti64-eli`); ti6al4v cutting.min_chip_thickness_ratio (`wojciechowski-2022-min-chip-thickness`); ti6al4v friction mu_sliding (carbide, uncoated) (`klippel-2024-ck45-orthogonal`).
* Material: database entry ti6al4v; the case says: Grade 5 ELI (extra-low interstitials) bar D 50 mm: Rm 902 MPa, Rp0.2 815 MPa, A 13 %, Z 49 %, 29 HRC (Table 1)
* Coolant 'Blaser Blasocut 2000 universal, 10 % emulsion' as emulsion-8pct; jet 157 m/s, 0.61 mm (Bernoulli at 150 bar, the stated flow by continuity), 20 mm from the corner, traced through the case's insert, holder and bar.
* Result: 36 compared, 0 without prediction, 0 not compared; median abs. error 40.4 %, max 54.9 %; 10/18 classes match; case **outside budget**; largest miss inf tolerances.
* Likely cause: scalar values: mean signed error +41 %; classes: 10 of 18 match. The jet reaches the cut in the case's scene; its lubrication, the cooling of the tool body and the hydraulic wedge are in the prediction. The remaining miss sits in the dry level of the mechanics (contact temperature, cutting force) and, for the chip grades, in the jet's push on the chip's back and the chip-breaker geometry, which are not modelled. The flank heat no longer offsets the jet: the coefficient pipeline's flank share is computed for the heat it is applied to (the friction on the wear land and the edge arc and the edge's plastic work) over the contact it is deposited on, and a cooled tool side of that contact runs cooler, not hotter (docs/models/mechanics.md 6); the jet moves the contact means little because the flank contact of a sharp, unworn edge is short against the rake contact.

### coolant-sultana2023-ti64-endmill-hpc-vs-dry

* Source: `sultana-2023-ti64-hpc-milling`, Sect. 2 and Table 4 (setup, nozzle data), Sect. 2 text (thermocouple calibration), Table 5 (L27 full factorial: T per run) (measured).
* File: `validation/cases/coolant_reach/coolant-sultana2023-ti64-endmill-hpc-vs-dry.yaml`; operation peripheral_milling; material `ti6al4v`.
* Validation class: **independent**.
* Model chain: cnctwin.coolant.occlusion (case scene: rotating cutter, block) -> cnctwin.coolant.engagement.face_h (jet cooling) -> cnctwin.coolant.lubrication.friction -> cnctwin.thermal.toolmodel.ToolThermal (tool body over the pass) -> cnctwin.mechanics.coefficients (contact mean temperature).
* Settings: coating = none, cut_length_mm = 100, edge_radius_um = 3, engagement_deg = 90, fluid = straight-oil, helix_deg = 30, hpc_jet_h_W_m2K = 2.451e+04, rhpc_jet_h_W_m2K = 1.777e+04, tool_material = hss-m42.
* Inputs the case marks as assumed: `setup.conditions.spindle_rpm` = [424, 637, 849] (derived n = 1000 vc / (pi x 12 mm); the CFD model used 850 rpm for 32 m/min).
* Inputs the harness assumed: tool material = hss-m42 (powder-metallurgical cobalt HSS: the HSS-Co (M42) entry, db/tool_materials); ambient temperature = 20 degC (case states none (or marks it assumed): ISO 1 reference temperature, est); cutting-edge radius = 3 um (not stated in the case: the tooling model's default for this tool (sharp_edge_radius_m), db/models/tooling.yaml); chip thickness = mean over the engagement, fz (1 - cos phi_e) / phi_e (a thermocouple average over the cut, est); cut length = 100 mm (not stated: one pass along the 100 mm block, est); end-mill stickout = 40 mm (not stated: the 27 mm flutes and 13 mm of shank out of the holder; holder from thermal's defaults, est); coolant fluid straight-oil = database entry (the case's fluid is not in the database: the closest database fluid, db/coolants); HPC jet direction = normal to the tool axis at mid-depth, 45 deg to the feed, from the cut's open side (the case shows the jet only in a schematic (Fig. 3), est); rotary applicator = 4 jets turning with the tool, each onto a rake face at normal incidence (the case: nozzles at 90 deg spacing aimed at the rake faces, case).
* Database values used: tool material hss-m42 thermal properties (`bohler-2026-s500`); ti6al4v flow stress (Johnson-Cook) (`ducobu-2017-jc-ti6al4v`); ti6al4v physical.density_kg_m3 (`aubert-duval-2018-ta6v`); ti6al4v physical.specific_heat_J_kgK (`upmet-2026-ti64-eli`); ti6al4v physical.conductivity_W_mK (`upmet-2026-ti64-eli`); ti6al4v cutting.min_chip_thickness_ratio (`wojciechowski-2022-min-chip-thickness`); ti6al4v friction mu_sliding (hss, uncoated) (`est`).
* Material: database entry ti6al4v; the case says: Ti-6Al-4V grade 5, bimodal alpha+beta, 37 HRC; convex-shaped block 100 x 50 x 25 mm
* Edge radius: an uncoated solid tool without a stated edge preparation has the ground (sharp) edge in the tooling model (tooling.common.edge_prep), not the light hone of coated solid tools.
* hpc: 1 jet(s) of 121 m/s, 0.59 mm (Bernoulli at 80 bar, the stated flow by continuity), Martin h 24511 W/m2K over 44% of the flutes, reach 0.86.
* rhpc: 4 jet(s) of 121 m/s, 0.30 mm (Bernoulli at 80 bar, the stated flow by continuity), Martin h 17771 W/m2K over 44% of the flutes, reach 1.00.
* Result: 27 compared, 0 without prediction, 0 not compared; median abs. error 12.5 %, max 67.0 %; case **outside budget**; largest miss 4.47 tolerances.
* Likely cause: scalar values: mean signed error +13 %. The jet reaches the cut in the case's scene; its lubrication, the cooling of the tool body and the hydraulic wedge are in the prediction. The remaining miss sits in the dry level of the mechanics (contact temperature, cutting force) and, for the chip grades, in the jet's push on the chip's back and the chip-breaker geometry, which are not modelled. The flank heat no longer offsets the jet: the coefficient pipeline's flank share is computed for the heat it is applied to (the friction on the wear land and the edge arc and the edge's plastic work) over the contact it is deposited on, and a cooled tool side of that contact runs cooler, not hotter (docs/models/mechanics.md 6); the jet moves the contact means little because the flank contact of a sharp, unworn edge is short against the rake contact.

## cutting_force

* Budget: main_force_pct 15 %, milling_peak_force_pct 20 %, thrust_force_pct 30 % (relative error of the simulated steady mean force component (turning, orthogonal cutting) or of the peak / extreme value of a force component (milling) against the reference value).
* Verdict: **fail** - only 0 of 17 cases within budget (0% < 80%); force-slot-in718-zheng2023-ballend misses by 39.00 tolerances (> 2).
* Harness: `cnctwin.validation.harnesses.cutforce.cutting_force`.
* The cutting domain's force model at the case's engagement: rows of the coefficient pipeline (MechanicsModel: Oxley / Johnson-Cook, calibrated to the material's measured Kienzle or mechanistic data of the pairing where the database has them) integrated by the domain's kernels over a prescribed steady cut (turn_step on a polar field for orthogonal tube turning, cut_step with analytic straight-cut engagement for milling); each reference statistic (mean, peak, extreme, RMS) taken from the simulated force on the tool.

| group | cases | cases within budget | counted values (no prediction) | median abs. error (all / values / curve points) | p90 abs. error | class matches | values within uncertainty | largest miss (tolerances) |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| independent | 17 | 0/17 | 295 (0) | 28.5 % / 26.3 % / 29.2 % | 91.6 % | - | 2/127 | 39 (force-slot-in718-zheng2023-ballend) |
| calibration-dependent | 8 | 0/8 | 254 (0) | 16.1 % / 16.1 % / - | 113.7 % | - | 18/196 | 11.6 (force-orth-ss304-marcicano2005-b) |

**Failures and likely causes.** 0 of 17 independent cases within budget. Values within tolerance per case: force-facemill-42crmo4-plodzien2023-toroidal 14/27; force-highfeed-ss1.4541-reznicek2023 24/36; force-mill-aisi1045-villarrazo2023-mql 0/2; force-orth-al6061-javidikia2021-feed 1/12; force-orth-al6061-javidikia2021-speed 4/16; force-orth-al7075-jomaa2014 0/8; force-orth-in718-diazalvarez2017 7/24; force-orth-in718-wood2020 3/6; force-orth-ti64-loschner2025 5/34; force-orth-ti64-slusarczyk2025 22/40; force-sidemill-al7075-zylka2023-edge-microgeometry 1/3; force-sidemill-in718-lin2021-annealed 14/36; force-sidemill-ss304-wang2025-hss 2/18; force-slot-in718-zheng2023-ballend 1/18; force-slot-ti64-engin-bullnose 0/3; force-slot-ti64-engin-taperball 0/3; force-thinwall-al7050-zawada2025-finish 0/9. Independent values: main_force_pct 15/70 values within tolerance, mean signed error +4 %; thrust_force_pct 27/70 values within tolerance, mean signed error +55 %; milling_peak_force_pct 56/155 values within tolerance, mean signed error -27 %. The thrust (feed) force is the weak part of the orthogonal model: it is set by the edge force below the stagnation point and the friction estimate, while the main force follows the flow stress. Milling cases add the tool geometry the sources leave open (rake, edge radius, entering angle: listed as assumptions per case). Cases whose material file holds Kienzle data of the same experiments are calibration-dependent and reported apart. No coefficient was changed for this report.

### force-facemill-42crmo4-plodzien2023-toroidal

* Source: `plodzien-2023-42crmo4-toroidal-face-milling`, Sect. 2.2 (setup), Table 2 (plan), Table 3 'Real' columns (measured forces) (measured, stated uncertainty 3 %).
* File: `validation/cases/cutting_force/force-facemill-42crmo4-plodzien2023-toroidal.yaml`; operation face_milling; material `aisi4140-annealed`.
* Validation class: **independent**.
* Model chain: cnctwin.mechanics.materials (databases) -> cnctwin.tooling.build_tool (edge model) -> cnctwin.cutting.coefficients.MechanicsModel (rows per coefficient group) -> cnctwin.cutting.kernels.cut_step (cnctwin.cutting.drive.Driver, analytic engaged intervals, prefilled surface memory).
* Settings: calibration = kienzle, calibration_sources = ["walter-2025-technical-compendium"], coating = tialn, edge_element_m = 0.00025, h_min_ratio = 0.27, mu_sliding = 0.4, process = milling, runout = 0, steps = <= 0.5 deg, >= 96 per tooth, tool_material = carbide-k20-6co, tool_type = toroidal_endmill.
* Inputs the case marks as assumed: `setup.tool.edge_radius_um` = 30 (assumed, not derived from a source: the edge was scanned for the FE model but no radius is given; 30 um is a judgement for a honed PVD-coated milling insert); `setup.conditions.radial_position` = partial immersion from the exit side: the insert exits the cut tangentially, engagement arc about 87 deg (assumed: the source says only 'milling was carried out concurrently' (down milling) with ae 20 mm; the position of the cutter axis relative to the workpiece edges is not stated); `setup.conditions.spindle_rpm` = 1743 (computed here from vc 230 m/min and D 42 mm (n = 1000 vc / (pi D)); not stated).
* Inputs the harness assumed: tool material = carbide-k20-6co (grade not in the database: the carbide class default, db/tool_materials); coating = tialn (case coating 'PVD TiAlN' mapped to the database coating tialn, db/coatings); ambient temperature = 20 degC (case states none (or marks it assumed): ISO 1 reference temperature, est); tool and work temperature = 20 degC (a test cut of seconds: tool body and work at ambient (the contact flash is in the mechanics heat partition), est); runout = 0 (not stated in the case: an ideal concentric tool, est).
* Database values used: tool material carbide-k20-6co thermal properties (`mitsubishi-2026-tm102g`); aisi4140-annealed flow stress (Johnson-Cook) (`est`); aisi4140-annealed physical.density_kg_m3 (`thyssenkrupp-2017-42crmo4`); aisi4140-annealed physical.specific_heat_J_kgK (`est`); aisi4140-annealed physical.conductivity_W_mK (`est`); aisi4140-annealed cutting.min_chip_thickness_ratio (`est`); aisi4140-annealed friction mu_sliding (carbide, TiAlN) (`est`); aisi4140-annealed cutting.indentation_coeff_N_mm3 (`est`); aisi4140-annealed cutting.kienzle (carbide, any, any) calibrating the forces (`walter-2025-technical-compendium`).
* Material: database entry aisi4140-annealed; the case says: 42CrMo4 at 220 HB (source Sect. 2.2; DB entry +A 200-241 HB); composition (Table 1, ranges): C 0.38-0.45, Mn 0.6-0.9, Si <= 0.4, Cr 0.9-1.2, Mo 0.15-0.3 %
* Tool model: core diameter not given: 0.50 D (core_ratio_by_flutes)
* Ff along the feed = X, FfN normal to the feed in the face plane = Y, Fa axial = Z; peak magnitudes (source frame signs not compared).
* Coefficients calibrated to the material's kienzle data (walter-2025-technical-compendium) for carbide/tialn, milling (cnctwin.mechanics build_calibration; Oxley carries the dependence away from the reference conditions).
* Result: 27 compared, 0 without prediction, 0 not compared; median abs. error 19.0 %, max 44.7 %; 2/27 within uncertainty; case **outside budget**; largest miss 2.23 tolerances.
* Likely cause: milling_peak_force_pct: 14/27 within, mean signed error -4 %. Coefficients: calibrated to kienzle data. Milling statistics are taken from a rigid tool and workpiece without runout; dynamometer dynamics, runout and the unstated frame orientations of some sources are not modelled.

### force-highfeed-ss1.4541-reznicek2023

* Source: `reznicek-2023-ss1.4541-high-feed-milling-forces`, Sect. 2 (material Tables 1-3, machine, tool, dynamometer, procedure), Table 4 (conditions), Tables 5, 8 and 9 (measured Fx, Fy, Fz at vc 160 m/min for ap 0.5, 0.6 and 0.7 mm) (measured).
* File: `validation/cases/cutting_force/force-highfeed-ss1.4541-reznicek2023.yaml`; operation face_milling; material `aisi316l`.
* Validation class: **independent**.
* Model chain: cnctwin.mechanics.materials (databases) -> cnctwin.tooling.build_tool (edge model) -> cnctwin.cutting.coefficients.MechanicsModel (rows per coefficient group) -> cnctwin.cutting.kernels.cut_step (cnctwin.cutting.drive.Driver, analytic engaged intervals, prefilled surface memory).
* Settings: calibration = kienzle, calibration_sources = ["walter-2025-technical-compendium"], coating = none, edge_element_m = 0.00025, h_min_ratio = 0.27, mu_sliding = 0.3, process = milling, runout = 0, steps = <= 0.5 deg, >= 96 per tooth, tool_material = carbide-k20-6co, tool_type = face_mill.
* Inputs the case marks as assumed: `setup.tool.entering_angle_deg` = 15 (assumed: not stated; high-feed heads cut with a small entering angle, 15 deg is a judgement, not a catalogue value); `setup.conditions.coolant` = dry (assumed: no coolant is mentioned in the source); `setup.conditions.radial_position` = centred over the 14 mm wide surface (assumed: not stated (grooves 5 mm thick were milled at both sides of each surface for clean entry and exit)); `setup.conditions.spindle_rpm` = 2546 (computed here from vc 160 m/min and D 20 mm).
* Inputs the harness assumed: insert edge length = 6.4 mm (the insert width LPHT0603 (case text: width 6.4 mm), case); tool material = carbide-k20-6co (grade not in the database: the carbide class default, db/tool_materials); coating = none (not stated in the case: uncoated assumed, case); ambient temperature = 20 degC (case states none (or marks it assumed): ISO 1 reference temperature, est); tool and work temperature = 20 degC (a test cut of seconds: tool body and work at ambient (the contact flash is in the mechanics heat partition), est); runout = 0 (not stated in the case: an ideal concentric tool, est).
* Database values used: tool material carbide-k20-6co thermal properties (`mitsubishi-2026-tm102g`); aisi316l flow stress (Johnson-Cook) (`benmeddour-2021-316l-turning`); aisi316l physical.density_kg_m3 (`outokumpu-steel-grades`); aisi316l physical.specific_heat_J_kgK (`outokumpu-steel-grades`); aisi316l physical.conductivity_W_mK (`outokumpu-steel-grades`); aisi316l cutting.min_chip_thickness_ratio (`est`); aisi316l friction mu_sliding (carbide, uncoated) (`benmeddour-2021-316l-turning`); aisi316l cutting.indentation_coeff_N_mm3 (`est`); aisi316l cutting.kienzle (carbide, any, any) calibrating the forces (`walter-2025-technical-compendium`).
* Material: database entry aisi316l; the case says: named 1.4541 (AISI 321, Ti-stabilised) with HB 205, Rp0.2 195 MPa, Rm 593 MPa, k 15 W/m K (Tables 2-3); its Table 1 composition (C 0.04, Cr 17.5, Ni 9.5, Mo 2.10, Ti 0.35 %) contains Mo and matches 1.4571 (316Ti) rather than 1.4541, so the Mo-bearing 316L DB entry is used
* Tool model: insert clearance not given: 7 deg (ISO symbol C)
* Tool model: edge radius not given: 35 um (no preparation default)
* Tool model: cutter body diameter not given: 0.80 D (chip pockets)
* Centred cut: strip y in [-ae/2, ae/2].
* Coefficients calibrated to the material's kienzle data (walter-2025-technical-compendium) for carbide/none, milling (cnctwin.mechanics build_calibration; Oxley carries the dependence away from the reference conditions).
* Result: 36 compared, 0 without prediction, 0 not compared; median abs. error 4.3 %, max 58.7 %; case **outside budget**; largest miss 2.94 tolerances.
* Likely cause: milling_peak_force_pct: 24/36 within, mean signed error -17 %. Coefficients: calibrated to kienzle data. Milling statistics are taken from a rigid tool and workpiece without runout; dynamometer dynamics, runout and the unstated frame orientations of some sources are not modelled.

### force-mill-aisi1045-villarrazo2023-mql

* Source: `villarrazo-2023-aisi1045-cryomql-milling`, Sect. 2 (setup, Table 2 cutting parameters, Eqs. 1-2 RMS definition), Table 3 (RMS cutting forces at 630, 5040, 6300 and 7245 mm of cut length) (measured).
* File: `validation/cases/cutting_force/force-mill-aisi1045-villarrazo2023-mql.yaml`; operation peripheral_milling; material `aisi1045`.
* Validation class: **independent**.
* Model chain: cnctwin.mechanics.materials (databases) -> cnctwin.tooling.build_tool (edge model) -> cnctwin.cutting.coefficients.MechanicsModel (rows per coefficient group) -> cnctwin.cutting.kernels.cut_step (cnctwin.cutting.drive.Driver, analytic engaged intervals, prefilled surface memory).
* Settings: calibration = kienzle, calibration_sources = ["klippel-2022-ck45-cutting-database"], coating = none, edge_element_m = 0.00025, h_min_ratio = 0.27, mu_sliding = 0.5, process = milling, runout = 0, steps = <= 0.5 deg, >= 96 per tooth, tool_material = carbide-k20-6co, tool_type = shoulder_mill.
* Inputs the case marks as assumed: `setup.tool.corner_radius_mm` = 1.2 (decoded from the insert code (12 = 1.2 mm corner radius per the usual AOMT coding); not stated in words); `setup.tool.rake_deg` = 10 (assumed: not stated (positive AOMT milling insert)); `setup.conditions.direction` = down (assumed: not stated).
* Inputs the harness assumed: insert edge length = 11 mm (AOMT 12 insert (12 mm class): usable edge about 11 mm, est); tool material = carbide-k20-6co (grade not in the database: the carbide class default, db/tool_materials); coating = none (not stated in the case: uncoated assumed, case); ambient temperature = 20 degC (case states none (or marks it assumed): ISO 1 reference temperature, est); tool and work temperature = 20 degC (a test cut of seconds: tool body and work at ambient (the contact flash is in the mechanics heat partition), est); coolant fluid mql-ester = database entry (the case's fluid is not in the database: the closest database fluid, full reach of the jet, db/coolants); runout = 0 (not stated in the case: an ideal concentric tool, est).
* Database values used: tool material carbide-k20-6co thermal properties (`mitsubishi-2026-tm102g`); aisi1045 flow stress (Johnson-Cook) (`burns-2012-1045-cutting-temperature`); aisi1045 physical.density_kg_m3 (`xiu-2019-1045-grinding-fe`); aisi1045 physical.specific_heat_J_kgK (`xiu-2019-1045-grinding-fe`); aisi1045 physical.conductivity_W_mK (`xiu-2019-1045-grinding-fe`); aisi1045 cutting.min_chip_thickness_ratio (`est`); aisi1045 friction mu_sliding (carbide, uncoated) (`est`); aisi1045 cutting.indentation_coeff_N_mm3 (`mal-2015-machining-course`); aisi1045 cutting.kienzle (carbide, uncoated, any) calibrating the forces (`klippel-2022-ck45-cutting-database`).
* Material: database entry aisi1045; the case says: AISI 1045 at 220 HB (Table 1: C 0.40-0.50, Mn 0.50-0.80, Si 0.15-0.40 %); harder than the normalised DB entry (190 HB)
* Tool model: edge radius not given: 35 um (no preparation default)
* Tool model: cutter body diameter not given: 0.80 D (chip pockets)
* MQL and CryoMQL get the same prediction: the CO2 of CryoMQL is not modelled; the MQL oil enters through the lubrication friction factor.
* Coefficients calibrated to the material's kienzle data (klippel-2022-ck45-cutting-database) for carbide/none, milling (cnctwin.mechanics build_calibration; Oxley carries the dependence away from the reference conditions).
* Result: 2 compared, 0 without prediction, 6 not compared; median abs. error 88.3 %, max 88.5 %; case **outside budget**; largest miss 4.43 tolerances.
* Likely cause: milling_peak_force_pct: 0/2 within, mean signed error +88 %. Coefficients: calibrated to kienzle data. Ck45 forms a built-up edge below about 70 m/min (database BUE window); the force rows carry no BUE. Milling statistics are taken from a rigid tool and workpiece without runout; dynamometer dynamics, runout and the unstated frame orientations of some sources are not modelled.

| F_RMS_CryoMQL_630mm | - | 179.3 | 337.2 | N | +88.0 % | 35.9 | **no** | rms3 , magnitude; 6000 rpm, fz 0.15, ap 3, ae 9 mm, down, mql |

### force-orth-al6061-javidikia2021-feed

* Source: `javidikia-2021-al6061-orthogonal`, Fig. 4 (feed series at 950 m/min) and Fig. 5 (speed series at 0.16 mm): values printed on the bars (measured).
* File: `validation/cases/cutting_force/force-orth-al6061-javidikia2021-feed.yaml`; operation orthogonal_turning; material `al6061-t6`.
* Validation class: **independent**.
* Model chain: cnctwin.mechanics.materials (databases) -> cnctwin.cutting.coefficients.MechanicsModel (Oxley / Johnson-Cook maps, edge and ploughing forces, calibration to measured Kienzle / mechanistic data of the pairing) -> cnctwin.cutting.coefficients.build_table -> cnctwin.cutting.kernels.turn_step (cnctwin.cutting.drive.Driver, polar field).
* Settings: calibration = kienzle, calibration_sources = ["iscar-materials-groups"], coating = none, edge_elements = 40, h_min_ratio = 0.23, mu_sliding = 0.55, process = turning, step_deg = 0.5, tool_material = carbide-k20-6co, tube_outer_radius_m = 0.075.
* Inputs the case marks as assumed: `setup.tool.nose_radius_mm` = 0.8 (assumed: ISO code '08' = 0.8 mm corner radius (code convention); outside the cut on a 4 mm tube wall).
* Inputs the harness assumed: tool material = carbide-k20-6co (grade not in the database: the carbide class default, db/tool_materials); ambient temperature = 20 degC (case states none (or marks it assumed): ISO 1 reference temperature, est); tool and work temperature = 20 degC (a test cut of seconds: tool body and work at ambient (the contact flash is in the mechanics heat partition), est); coolant fluid emulsion-8pct = database entry (the case's fluid is not in the database: the closest database fluid, full reach of the jet, db/coolants).
* Database values used: tool material carbide-k20-6co thermal properties (`mitsubishi-2026-tm102g`); al6061-t6 flow stress (Johnson-Cook) (`lesuer-2001-llnl-jc`); al6061-t6 physical.density_kg_m3 (`kaiser-2006-al6061-datasheet`); al6061-t6 physical.specific_heat_J_kgK (`muraca-1972-al6061-handbook`); al6061-t6 physical.conductivity_W_mK (`kaiser-2006-al6061-datasheet`); al6061-t6 cutting.min_chip_thickness_ratio (`wojciechowski-2022-min-chip-thickness`); al6061-t6 friction mu_sliding (carbide, uncoated) (`est`); al6061-t6 cutting.indentation_coeff_N_mm3 (`est`); al6061-t6 cutting.kienzle (carbide, any, any) calibrating the forces (`iscar-materials-groups`).
* Material: database entry al6061-t6; the case says: AA6061-T6 cylinder, 150 mm diameter, 120 mm long
* Coefficients calibrated to the material's kienzle data (iscar-materials-groups) for carbide/none, turning (cnctwin.mechanics build_calibration; Oxley carries the dependence away from the reference conditions).
* The kernel value (turn_step) is compared; 'closed_form' in the extras is the rows' (K h + Ke) b at the same h (a check of the table and the element integration, normally within 1-2 %).
* Result: 12 compared, 0 without prediction, 0 not compared; median abs. error 28.9 %, max 70.1 %; case **outside budget**; largest miss 2.34 tolerances.
* Likely cause: main_force_pct: 1/6 within, mean signed error +18 %; thrust_force_pct: 0/6 within, mean signed error +51 %. Coefficients: calibrated to kienzle data.

### force-orth-al7075-jomaa2014

* Source: `jomaa-2014-al7075-cutting-table`, Table 2 (experimental results of the orthogonal machining), Table 3 (conditions) (measured).
* File: `validation/cases/cutting_force/force-orth-al7075-jomaa2014.yaml`; operation orthogonal_turning; material `al7075-t6`.
* Validation class: **independent**.
* Model chain: cnctwin.mechanics.materials (databases) -> cnctwin.cutting.coefficients.MechanicsModel (Oxley / Johnson-Cook maps, edge and ploughing forces, calibration to measured Kienzle / mechanistic data of the pairing) -> cnctwin.cutting.coefficients.build_table -> cnctwin.cutting.kernels.turn_step (cnctwin.cutting.drive.Driver, polar field).
* Settings: calibration = kienzle, calibration_sources = ["iscar-materials-groups"], coating = none, edge_elements = 40, h_min_ratio = 0.3, mu_sliding = 0.55, process = turning, step_deg = 0.5, tool_material = carbide-k20-6co, tube_outer_radius_m = 1.
* Inputs the case marks as assumed: `setup.tool.clearance_deg` = 5 (assumed: not stated: a TNMA insert has 0 deg own clearance, tilted 5 deg negative in the holder); `setup.tool.edge_radius_um` = 30 (assumed: not stated; TNMA inserts are edge-honed (typically 25-50 um, est.)); `setup.tool.nose_radius_mm` = 0.8 (assumed: ISO code '08' = 0.8 mm corner radius (code convention)); `setup.conditions.width_of_cut_mm` = 4 (assumed: not stated which dimension is the width; the disc thickness (4 mm) is assumed).
* Inputs the harness assumed: tool material = carbide-k20-6co (grade not in the database: the carbide class default, db/tool_materials); ambient temperature = 20 degC (case states none (or marks it assumed): ISO 1 reference temperature, est); tool and work temperature = 20 degC (a test cut of seconds: tool body and work at ambient (the contact flash is in the mechanics heat partition), est); cut geometry = straight edge on a 1 m radius (disc periphery or planing: the cutting speed is uniform across the width, est).
* Database values used: tool material carbide-k20-6co thermal properties (`mitsubishi-2026-tm102g`); al7075-t6 flow stress (Johnson-Cook) (`luo-2021-al7075-drilling`); al7075-t6 physical.density_kg_m3 (`muraca-1972-al7075-handbook`); al7075-t6 physical.specific_heat_J_kgK (`muraca-1972-al7075-handbook`); al7075-t6 physical.conductivity_W_mK (`muraca-1972-al7075-handbook`); al7075-t6 cutting.min_chip_thickness_ratio (`wojciechowski-2022-min-chip-thickness`); al7075-t6 friction mu_sliding (carbide, uncoated) (`luo-2021-al7075-drilling`); al7075-t6 cutting.indentation_coeff_N_mm3 (`est`); al7075-t6 cutting.kienzle (carbide, any, any) calibrating the forces (`iscar-materials-groups`).
* Material: database entry al7075-t6; the case says: AA7075-T651 discs (T6 plus stress relief by stretching)
* Coefficients calibrated to the material's kienzle data (iscar-materials-groups) for carbide/none, turning (cnctwin.mechanics build_calibration; Oxley carries the dependence away from the reference conditions).
* The kernel value (turn_step) is compared; 'closed_form' in the extras is the rows' (K h + Ke) b at the same h (a check of the table and the element integration, normally within 1-2 %).
* Result: 8 compared, 0 without prediction, 0 not compared; median abs. error 72.2 %, max 146.6 %; case **outside budget**; largest miss 4.89 tolerances.
* Likely cause: main_force_pct: 0/4 within, mean signed error +38 %; thrust_force_pct: 0/4 within, mean signed error +115 %. Coefficients: calibrated to kienzle data.

### force-orth-in718-diazalvarez2017

* Source: `diazalvarez-2017-in718-orthogonal`, Fig. 8a (cutting force) and Fig. 8b (thrust force) vs cutting speed, markers digitised from the 737 x 231 px figure (5.6 N/px in a, 4.0 N/px in b) (measured, digitised, stated uncertainty 3 %).
* File: `validation/cases/cutting_force/force-orth-in718-diazalvarez2017.yaml`; operation orthogonal_turning; material `inconel718`.
* Validation class: **independent**.
* Model chain: cnctwin.mechanics.materials (databases) -> cnctwin.cutting.coefficients.MechanicsModel (Oxley / Johnson-Cook maps, edge and ploughing forces, calibration to measured Kienzle / mechanistic data of the pairing) -> cnctwin.cutting.coefficients.build_table -> cnctwin.cutting.kernels.turn_step (cnctwin.cutting.drive.Driver, polar field).
* Settings: calibration = kienzle, calibration_sources = ["walter-2025-technical-compendium"], coating = none, edge_elements = 40, h_min_ratio = 0.3, mu_sliding = 0.3, process = turning, step_deg = 0.5, tool_material = carbide-k20-6co, tube_outer_radius_m = 1.
* Inputs the case marks as assumed: `setup.tool.nose_radius_mm` = 0.8 (assumed: ISO code '08' = 0.8 mm corner radius (code convention); outside the cut on a 2 mm disc).
* Inputs the harness assumed: tool material = carbide-k20-6co (db/tool_materials/carbide-k20-6co.yaml names Sandvik H13A (straight WC-6Co), db/tool_materials); ambient temperature = 20 degC (case states none (or marks it assumed): ISO 1 reference temperature, est); tool and work temperature = 20 degC (a test cut of seconds: tool body and work at ambient (the contact flash is in the mechanics heat partition), est); cut geometry = straight edge on a 1 m radius (disc periphery or planing: the cutting speed is uniform across the width, est).
* Database values used: tool material carbide-k20-6co thermal properties (`mitsubishi-2026-tm102g`); inconel718 flow stress (Johnson-Cook) (`qiu-2019-in718-jc-sensitivity`); inconel718 physical.density_kg_m3 (`special-metals-2007-in718`); inconel718 physical.specific_heat_J_kgK (`wang-2018-in718-chip-formation`); inconel718 physical.conductivity_W_mK (`special-metals-2007-in718`); inconel718 cutting.min_chip_thickness_ratio (`est`); inconel718 friction mu_sliding (carbide, PVD) (`qiu-2019-in718-jc-sensitivity`); inconel718 cutting.indentation_coeff_N_mm3 (`est`); inconel718 cutting.kienzle (carbide, any, any) calibrating the forces (`walter-2025-technical-compendium`).
* Material: database entry inconel718; the case says: Inconel 718 disc; heat-treatment condition not stated ('heat-treatable Inconel 718 disk', Sect. 2.3)
* Coefficients calibrated to the material's kienzle data (walter-2025-technical-compendium) for carbide/none, turning (cnctwin.mechanics build_calibration; Oxley carries the dependence away from the reference conditions).
* The kernel value (turn_step) is compared; 'closed_form' in the extras is the rows' (K h + Ke) b at the same h (a check of the table and the element integration, normally within 1-2 %).
* Result: 24 compared, 0 without prediction, 0 not compared; median abs. error 30.7 %, max 52.3 %; 0/24 within uncertainty; case **outside budget**; largest miss 3.17 tolerances.
* Likely cause: main_force_pct: 0/12 within, mean signed error +34 %; thrust_force_pct: 7/12 within, mean signed error +25 %. Coefficients: calibrated to kienzle data.

### force-orth-ss304-marcicano2005-b

* Source: `marcicano-2005-aisi304-cutting-tests`, Table 4 (insert of maker B, width 1.5 mm): Fa, Fc, Vc, t0 (measured).
* File: `validation/cases/cutting_force/force-orth-ss304-marcicano2005-b.yaml`; operation orthogonal_turning; material `aisi304`.
* Validation class: **calibration-dependent**: aisi304 cutting.kienzle (carbide, uncoated, any) calibrating the forces from `marcicano-2005-304-machining` (named in its note).
* Model chain: cnctwin.mechanics.materials (databases) -> cnctwin.cutting.coefficients.MechanicsModel (Oxley / Johnson-Cook maps, edge and ploughing forces, calibration to measured Kienzle / mechanistic data of the pairing) -> cnctwin.cutting.coefficients.build_table -> cnctwin.cutting.kernels.turn_step (cnctwin.cutting.drive.Driver, polar field).
* Settings: calibration = kienzle, calibration_sources = ["est"], coating = none, edge_elements = 40, h_min_ratio = 0.27, mu_sliding = 0.6, process = turning, step_deg = 0.5, tool_material = carbide-k20-6co, tube_outer_radius_m = 0.025.
* Inputs the case marks as assumed: `setup.tool.clearance_deg` = 6 (assumed: not stated: ISO 1832 letter P = 11 deg insert clearance (code convention, standard not read) minus the 5 deg positive tilt that gives the stated rake); `setup.tool.edge_radius_um` = 15 (assumed: not stated; typical of a ground (G-tolerance) uncoated insert edge (est.)); `setup.tool.nose_radius_mm` = 0.8 (assumed: ISO code '08' = 0.8 mm corner radius (code convention); outside the cut when the tube wall lies on the straight edge); `setup.conditions.coolant` = dry (assumed: not stated; dry orthogonal tests are implied by the analysis).
* Inputs the harness assumed: tool material = carbide-k20-6co (grade not in the database: the carbide class default, db/tool_materials); ambient temperature = 20 degC (case states none (or marks it assumed): ISO 1 reference temperature, est); tool and work temperature = 20 degC (a test cut of seconds: tool body and work at ambient (the contact flash is in the mechanics heat partition), est); tube outer diameter = 50 mm (tube diameter not stated: only the speed variation across the width depends on it, harnesses/assumptions.py).
* Database values used: tool material carbide-k20-6co thermal properties (`mitsubishi-2026-tm102g`); aisi304 flow stress (Johnson-Cook) (`zou-2026-304-min-cutting-thickness`); aisi304 physical.density_kg_m3 (`thyssenkrupp-2020-14301`); aisi304 physical.specific_heat_J_kgK (`thyssenkrupp-2020-14301`); aisi304 physical.conductivity_W_mK (`outokumpu-steel-grades`); aisi304 cutting.min_chip_thickness_ratio (`zou-2026-304-min-cutting-thickness`); aisi304 friction mu_sliding (carbide, uncoated) (`zou-2026-304-min-cutting-thickness`); aisi304 cutting.indentation_coeff_N_mm3 (`est`); aisi304 cutting.kienzle (carbide, uncoated, any) calibrating the forces (`est`).
* Material: database entry aisi304; the case says: annealed AISI 304 tubes; quasi-static compression gives JC A 360 MPa, B 1244 MPa, n 0.72 (Table 1); hardness not stated
* Coefficients calibrated to the material's kienzle data (est) for carbide/none, turning (cnctwin.mechanics build_calibration; Oxley carries the dependence away from the reference conditions).
* The kernel value (turn_step) is compared; 'closed_form' in the extras is the rows' (K h + Ke) b at the same h (a check of the table and the element integration, normally within 1-2 %).
* Result: 26 compared, 0 without prediction, 0 not compared; median abs. error 107.0 %, max 349.3 %; case **outside budget**; largest miss 11.6 tolerances.
* Likely cause: main_force_pct: 3/13 within, mean signed error +63 %; thrust_force_pct: 0/13 within, mean signed error +185 %. Coefficients: calibrated to kienzle data.

* Settings: calibration = kienzle, calibration_sources = ["iscar-materials-groups"], coating = none, edge_element_m = 0.00025, h_min_ratio = 0.3, mu_sliding = 0.55, process = milling, runout = 0, tool_material = carbide-k20-6co, tool_type = square_endmill.

### force-sidemill-ss304-wang2025-hss

* Source: `wang-2025-304-milling-force-wear`, Sect. 3 (setup), Table 1 (tool), Table 5 (tests), Fig. 9 a-f (experimental average forces, values printed on the bars) (measured, stated uncertainty 1 %).

* Settings: calibration = none, calibration_sources = [], coating = none, edge_element_m = 0.00025, h_min_ratio = 0.27, mu_sliding = 0.6, process = milling, runout = 0, steps = <= 0.5 deg, >= 96 per tooth, tool_material = hss-m2, tool_type = square_endmill.
* Inputs the case marks as assumed: `setup.tool.edge_radius_um` = 10 (assumed, not derived from a source: sharp ground HSS edge); `setup.tool.stickout_mm` = 30 (assumed: not stated); `setup.conditions.coolant` = dry (assumed: no coolant is mentioned in the source).
* Inputs the harness assumed: tool material = hss-m2 (grade not in the database: the hss class default, db/tool_materials); ambient temperature = 20 degC (case states none (or marks it assumed): ISO 1 reference temperature, est); tool and work temperature = 20 degC (a test cut of seconds: tool body and work at ambient (the contact flash is in the mechanics heat partition), est); runout = 0 (not stated in the case: an ideal concentric tool, est).
* Database values used: tool material hss-m2 thermal properties (`bohler-2026-s600`); aisi304 flow stress (Johnson-Cook) (`zou-2026-304-min-cutting-thickness`); aisi304 physical.density_kg_m3 (`thyssenkrupp-2020-14301`); aisi304 physical.specific_heat_J_kgK (`thyssenkrupp-2020-14301`); aisi304 physical.conductivity_W_mK (`outokumpu-steel-grades`); aisi304 cutting.min_chip_thickness_ratio (`zou-2026-304-min-cutting-thickness`); aisi304 friction mu_sliding (carbide, uncoated) (`zou-2026-304-min-cutting-thickness`); aisi304 cutting.indentation_coeff_N_mm3 (`est`).
* Material: database entry aisi304; the case says: 304 stainless steel; condition, hardness and supplier are not given in the source
* Tool model: core diameter not given: 0.60 D (core_ratio_by_flutes)
* Source X (normal to the feed) = Y here, source Y (feed) = X; magnitudes.
* Coefficients: the mechanics prediction alone (no measured Kienzle or mechanistic data in the material file for hss/none, milling).
* Result: 18 compared, 0 without prediction, 0 not compared; median abs. error 78.7 %, max 707.3 %; 0/18 within uncertainty; case **outside budget**; largest miss 35.4 tolerances.

* Coefficients calibrated to the material's kienzle data (iscar-materials-groups) for carbide/none, milling (cnctwin.mechanics build_calibration; Oxley carries the dependence away from the reference conditions).
* Result: 9 compared, 0 without prediction, 0 not compared; median abs. error 71.0 %, max 76.2 %; 0/9 within uncertainty; case **outside budget**; largest miss 3.81 tolerances.
* Likely cause: milling_peak_force_pct: 0/9 within, mean signed error -64 %. Coefficients: calibrated to kienzle data. Milling statistics are taken from a rigid tool and workpiece without runout; dynamometer dynamics, runout and the unstated frame orientations of some sources are not modelled.

## cycle_time

* Budget: 5 % (relative error of the simulated cycle time of the stated path, feed and controller limits).
* Verdict: **fail** - only 0 of 2 cases within budget (0% < 80%); cycletime-wilkinson2025-trochoid-tnc640-r5-r10 misses by 9.30 tolerances (> 2).
* Harness: `cnctwin.validation.harnesses.cycletime.cycle_time`.
* The case's toolpath (ISO dialect) through the controller executor and the motion planner and feed drives (MotionRunner, no cutting) on a three-axis machine with the stated interpolation period, acceleration, jerk, feed and contour tolerance; time from the first to the last path block.

Values within tolerance per case: cycletime-wilkinson2025-trochoid-840d-tnc640 2/8; cycletime-wilkinson2025-trochoid-tnc640-r5-r10 4/8. The program is the case's path in ISO syntax; the differences between the controls are carried only by their stated interpolation period, acceleration and jerk limits.

### cycletime-wilkinson2025-trochoid-840d-tnc640

* Source: `wilkinson-2025-fir-trochoidal`, Case study #2: text, Fig. 11b (plan view of the path, digitised), Table 3 (interpolator limits), Table 4 (cycle times), Listings 2-3 (NC headers) (measured, digitised, stated uncertainty 2 %).
* File: `validation/cases/cycle_time/cycletime-wilkinson2025-trochoid-840d-tnc640.yaml`; operation machine_motion; material `None`.
* Validation class: **independent**.
* Model chain: cnctwin.controller.Controller (FANUC dialect) -> cnctwin.motion.MotionDomain planner + drives -> cnctwin.motion.driver.MotionRunner.
* Settings: controller_db = fanuc-31i-b, template_machine = dmg-dmu50-3rd-siemens, tick_s = 1e-06.
* Inputs the harness assumed: G01 polygon of the 840D path = chordal deviation 5 um (segment length not stated (CAM output), est); drive data (gains, masses, rapid, max feed) = dmg-dmu50-3rd-siemens (not stated: the database machine of the same control family, db/machines); motor force limit = 10 x moving mass x the stated acceleration (the measured machine follows its control's limits; the template's own motor forces saturate at the case's jerk (excess following error). The cycle time does not change once the force is not binding (4x and 10x give the same time), est); corner mode = continuous path (blend within the tolerance) (the programs run in G64 / CYCLE832 and CYCL DEF 32 HSC mode (case settings); the template control's exact-stop default is not used, case); look-ahead depth and block processing time = 500 blocks, 1.0 ms (not stated: dmg-dmu50-3rd-siemens controller entries (est), db/machines).
* Program written in the ISO (FANUC) dialect from the case's path description; the Siemens/Heidenhain syntax of the sources is not implemented by cnctwin.controller.
* Result: 8 compared, 0 without prediction, 0 not compared; median abs. error 7.0 %, max 17.0 %; 2/8 within uncertainty; case **outside budget**; largest miss 3.4 tolerances.
* Likely cause: errors -17 to +2 %. The planner is a time-optimal jerk-limited profile with tolerance-band corner blends; the controls' own smoothing filters (CYCLE832/COMPCAD, cycle 32 HSC) slow the feed on the circle transitions and short segments more than the bounds alone (motion.md 9).

## drilling_thrust

* Budget: 20 % (relative error of the simulated steady thrust (axial) force during full-diameter drilling).
* Verdict: **fail** - only 0 of 4 cases within budget (0% < 80%); thrust-drill-ti64-beranoagirre2018-pilot misses by 4.71 tolerances (> 2).
* Harness: `cnctwin.validation.harnesses.drilling.drilling_thrust`.
* Steady full-diameter drilling through the cutting domain's kernel with the case's twist drill (tooling: lips with the helicoid rake of the stated helix, chisel, margins) and the coefficient pipeline for the process drilling (indentation model at the chisel and the slow centre); thrust = mean axial force on the drill.

| group | cases | cases within budget | counted values (no prediction) | median abs. error (all / values / curve points) | p90 abs. error | class matches | values within uncertainty | largest miss (tolerances) |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| independent | 4 | 0/4 | 49 (0) | 43.9 % / 18.6 % / 47.3 % | 91.7 % | - | 0/9 | 4.71 (thrust-drill-ti64-beranoagirre2018-pilot) |
| calibration-dependent | 1 | 0/1 | 9 (0) | 58.7 % / - / 58.7 % | 69.4 % | - | 0/9 | 3.62 (thrust-drill-al7075-luo2021) |

**Failures and likely causes.** 0 of 4 independent cases within budget. Values within tolerance per case: thrust-drill-aisi1040-gok2013-helix 1/3; thrust-drill-al2024-craciun2024-hss 9/27; thrust-drill-c45-sklenicka2024-carbide 4/9; thrust-drill-ti64-beranoagirre2018-pilot 0/10. Mean signed error over the independent values -0 %. Most sources leave the web, chisel angle, helix or point angle open (listed per case as assumed inputs); the thrust depends on the chisel length almost linearly (chisel indentation).

* Source: `luo-2021-al7075-drilling-thrust-torque`, Fig. 8a-c, 'Experimental' bars (digitised; 8.4, 10.8 and 11.9 N/px) (measured, digitised, stated uncertainty 2 %).
* File: `validation/cases/drilling_thrust/thrust-drill-al7075-luo2021.yaml`; operation drilling; material `al7075-t6`.
* Validation class: **calibration-dependent**: al7075-t6 flow stress (Johnson-Cook) from `luo-2021-al7075-drilling` (source); al7075-t6 friction mu_sliding (carbide, uncoated) from `luo-2021-al7075-drilling` (source).
* Model chain: cnctwin.mechanics.materials (databases) -> cnctwin.tooling.build_tool (twist drill: lips, chisel, margins) -> cnctwin.cutting.coefficients.MechanicsModel (process drilling: lips by Oxley, chisel and slow centre by indentation, calibration to measured data of the pairing) -> cnctwin.cutting.kernels.cut_step (steady full-diameter drilling, cnctwin.cutting.drive.Driver).
* Settings: calibration = kienzle, calibration_sources = ["iscar-materials-groups"], coating = none, edge_element_m = 0.00025, h_min_ratio = 0.3, mu_sliding = 0.55, process = drilling, statistic = mean over one revolution of the steady cut, tool_material = carbide-k20-6co.
* Inputs the case marks as assumed: `setup.tool.chisel_edge_angle_deg` = 125 (assumed: not stated; typical conventional point (est.)); `setup.tool.lip_count` = 2 (assumed: not stated; a standard two-flute twist drill is assumed); `setup.tool.web_thickness_mm` = 1.5 (assumed: not stated; ~0.15 D, typical of a standard jobber drill (est.)); `setup.conditions.coolant` = dry (assumed: not stated).
* Inputs the harness assumed: tool material = carbide-k20-6co (grade not in the database: the carbide class default, db/tool_materials); coating = none (not stated in the case: uncoated assumed, case); ambient temperature = 20 degC (case states none (or marks it assumed): ISO 1 reference temperature, est); tool and work temperature = 20 degC (a test cut of seconds: tool body and work at ambient (the contact flash is in the mechanics heat partition), est); cutting-edge radius = 3.0 um (not stated in the case: tooling's default for the drill's edge preparation, db/models/tooling.yaml); runout = 0 (not stated in the case: an ideal concentric drill, est).
* Database values used: tool material carbide-k20-6co thermal properties (`mitsubishi-2026-tm102g`); al7075-t6 flow stress (Johnson-Cook) (`luo-2021-al7075-drilling`); al7075-t6 physical.density_kg_m3 (`muraca-1972-al7075-handbook`); al7075-t6 physical.specific_heat_J_kgK (`muraca-1972-al7075-handbook`); al7075-t6 physical.conductivity_W_mK (`muraca-1972-al7075-handbook`); al7075-t6 cutting.min_chip_thickness_ratio (`wojciechowski-2022-min-chip-thickness`); al7075-t6 friction mu_sliding (carbide, uncoated) (`luo-2021-al7075-drilling`); al7075-t6 cutting.indentation_coeff_N_mm3 (`est`); al7075-t6 cutting.kienzle (carbide, any, any) calibrating the forces (`iscar-materials-groups`).
* Material: database entry al7075-t6; the case says: 7075-T6 workpiece (application: space-station suspended-ball structure); form, thickness and clamping identical for all tests but not given in numbers
* Drill model (D 10 mm): lip clearance not given: 10 deg
* Drill model (D 10 mm): edge radius not given: 3 um (no preparation default)
* Drill model (D 10 mm): margin width not given: 0.70 mm
* Coefficients calibrated to the material's kienzle data (iscar-materials-groups) for carbide/none, drilling (cnctwin.mechanics build_calibration; Oxley carries the dependence away from the reference conditions).

## drilling_torque

* Budget: 15 % (relative error of the simulated steady drilling torque).
* Verdict: **fail** - only 0 of 4 cases within budget (0% < 80%); torque-drill-ti64-beranoagirre2018-pilot misses by 6.11 tolerances (> 2).
* Harness: `cnctwin.validation.harnesses.drilling.drilling_torque`.
* As drilling_thrust; torque = mean process torque about the drill axis.

| group | cases | cases within budget | counted values (no prediction) | median abs. error (all / values / curve points) | p90 abs. error | class matches | values within uncertainty | largest miss (tolerances) |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| independent | 4 | 0/4 | 68 (0) | 24.4 % / 36.7 % / 21.3 % | 82.1 % | - | 1/31 | 6.11 (torque-drill-ti64-beranoagirre2018-pilot) |
| calibration-dependent | 1 | 0/1 | 9 (0) | 65.0 % / - / 65.0 % | 71.0 % | - | 0/9 | 4.86 (torque-drill-al7075-luo2021) |

**Failures and likely causes.** 0 of 4 independent cases within budget. Values within tolerance per case: torque-drill-42crmo4-krivokapic2020-hss 0/9; torque-drill-al2024-craciun2024-hss 21/27; torque-drill-c45-hnatik2025-carbide 2/22; torque-drill-ti64-beranoagirre2018-pilot 0/10. Mean signed error over the independent values -25 %. Most sources leave the web, chisel angle, helix or point angle open (listed per case as assumed inputs); the torque comes mostly from the lips, whose rake follows the helix.

* Source: `hnatik-2025-c45-drilling-torque`, Sects. 2.1-2.3 (material, machine, tools, constant factors), Table 2 (factor levels), Sect. 3 and Fig. 8 a-d (torque Mc versus fn for C45, black squares with error bars; digitised), Sect. 3 text (feed-averaged Mc) (measured, digitised, stated uncertainty 2 %).

* Validation class: **independent**.
